## Completion checks and evidence

### UX-002: Complete reference UI inventory

**In progress** · Next · Updated 2026-10-30

Inspect the installed reference app screen by screen: menus, tools, buttons, properties, dialogs, capture controls, and library. Map every observed control to a feature and evidence.

Completion: Record a coverage checklist, screenshots and menu/control inventory, distinguish inspected from uninspected surfaces, and resolve all inventory gaps before claiming a complete scan.

- Evidence: 53 local surfaces/states recorded, including20 named tool panels, File New/Import/Print, top/nested menus and five resize surfaces. Detailed dialogs, capture controls, video and full per-control inventory remain incomplete.
- Evidence: Bounded screenshots and observed-control ledger: artifacts/reference/ui/. Coverage and badge contract: docs/ui-parity.md.
- Evidence: Editor inspected at 981x687 and 1920x1028; toolbar overflow changes while right pane remains approximately 262 pixels wide.
- Evidence: Detailed audit maps233 controls from187 entries across23/56 recorded surfaces;97 are native verified. Arrow presets, width, opacity and custom save have scoped evidence. Themes, alternate endpoints, shadows and full reference parity remain unfinished.
- Evidence: Alpha.19 adds five directly observed resize surfaces and their per-control mapping. Reference flatten warning exposes a documented behavior difference; planned print/canvas controls remain red. docs/resize-reference-observations.md and docs/ui-controls.md.
- Evidence: 2026-10-30 planning refresh: prior provisional target passed while this scope remained unfinished. Target revised; no completion claim.
- Evidence: Alpha.21 adds the complete top-level Image menu mapping, distinguishing verified resize/Flatten All from missing selected flatten, transforms and other tools. docs/ui-controls.md.
- Evidence: 2026-10-30 planning refresh: alpha.29 prioritizes the observed arrow stroke defect; this unfinished provisional target is rescheduled by priority.
- Evidence: Alpha.32 maps Rotate menu and newly observed custom-angle dialog, distinguishing whole-image quarter turns, custom canvas angles and annotation rotation. Numeric reference entry remains unqualified. docs/ui-controls.md.
- Evidence: Alpha.33 candidate maps Crop overlay and observed Cancel restoration. The verified local workflow remains unreleased because of the Site archive size limit. docs/ui-controls.md.
- Evidence: 2026-10-30 user supplied four Arrow/Text/Callout/Shape properties screenshots. Read as user-provided visual reference only, not direct installed-app observation. Strong section bars/group dividers and Tool Properties/Objects tabs prepared; full control/behavior parity remains incomplete. docs/properties-refresh-work.md.
- Original target: 0.1.0-alpha.4 — OVERDUE; scope still unfinished
- Recorded target history: 0.1.0-alpha.4 → 0.1.0-alpha.5 → 0.1.0-alpha.6 → 0.1.0-alpha.7 → 0.1.0-alpha.8 → 0.1.0-alpha.9 → 0.1.0-alpha.10 → 0.1.0-alpha.12 → 0.1.0-alpha.13 → 0.1.0-alpha.14 → 0.1.0-alpha.20 → 0.1.0-alpha.28
- Release not scheduled; priorities are not version promises.

### UX-003: Match the reference application's layout

**In progress** · Next · Updated 2026-10-30

Match the observed layout and interaction states with original Rust code and ClipCraft branding. Alpha.5 integrates the title/menu row and expands nested menus; detailed properties, dialogs, capture, library and video views remain incomplete.

Completion: Compare each mapped screen at matching window size and DPI, verify control positions and interaction states, and record remaining visual and behavioral differences.

- Evidence: First shell verified at 981x687 and 1252x882: top menus, responsive tool strip/More, centered canvas, fixed right pane, Effects/Properties controls.
- Evidence: Native arrow creation, movement, Undo/Redo, text-dialog access, Save As, and exact PNG export passed in the relocated controls. docs/shell-verification.md.
- Evidence: Alpha.5: integrated title/menu row, title dragging, horizontal/vertical resize, minimize, maximize/restore and protected close verified natively. File Import/Print and Image Arrange/Align submenus inspected. docs/chrome-verification.md.
- Evidence: Windows snap layouts and drag-to-restore from maximized remain missing; use the restore button or title double-click. Other DPI and complete per-screen geometry remain pending.
- Evidence: Alpha.6 adds a working New Image dialog and a workspace-only checkerboard. The explicit transparency checkbox and egui color picker still differ from the observed reference structure.
- Evidence: Control audit separates behavior from layout: Text still uses a modal; Alpha.8 adds selected-text/callout Advanced disclosures and the New Image title close button; full property geometry remains incomplete. Combined Callout property rows are decomposed into working and missing controls. No new native inspection or full-layout parity is claimed.

- Evidence: Alpha.8 sidebar: selected-object properties, two bundled fonts, size, text RGBA, Advanced alignment and callout padding verified natively. One-step text alignment Undo/Redo and exact 256000-pixel native clipboard comparison passed. Full reference parity and richer formatting remain incomplete. docs/alpha8-properties-verification.md.

### UX-004: Temporary readiness and target-version badges

**In progress** · Next · Updated 2026-10-30

Temporary green Ready and red target-version labels now cover the first editor shell. Extend explicit scope/evidence mappings as remaining controls and dialogs are implemented.

Completion: Verify every displayed badge against evidence and the current build, map unfinished controls to explicit target versions, keep labels legible without obscuring controls, and provide a way to hide the temporary overlay.

- Evidence: Canonical inventory supplies build-local target labels; a separate native-verified scope list gates green labels. Status alone cannot imply readiness.
- Evidence: View and the bottom Badges checkbox hide the overlay. Exports with badges on/off and CLI output match all 576000 pixels; saving after toggling preserves the project checksum.
- Evidence: Planned Callout selection followed by drawing leaves the project unchanged. More exposes all 20 surveyed tools plus existing Redact.
- Evidence: Full per-control mapping and remaining UI surfaces are still incomplete. docs/shell-verification.md.
- Evidence: Alpha.5 replaces grouped placeholders with individual red targets in Import, Print, Arrange/Align and Rotate. Unknown/partial behavior never becomes green. Full coverage remains in progress.
